- **2026-06-13 — incircle: "circle inscribed in a triangle" (ADR-027).** Operator crash: `circle … inscribed in triangle ABC` (LLM incircle request, with a bogus `radius r`) → degenerate "A and I at the same point". Cause: `inscribedPolygon` matched "inscribed"+triangle and built polygon-in-circle (wrong) — but a *circle* inscribed in a *triangle* is the INCIRCLE. Fix: discriminate by ORDER (circle word before polygon word = incircle; `isCircleInPolygon`), and a new `incircle` rule builds it from primitives — two `bisector`s → `line-intersection` (incenter I) → `foot` from I to AB (inradius) → `circle-through`. `triangle inscribed in a circle` still routes correctly. Verified tangent to all 3 sides. Deferred: tangential-quad incircle (thm 88). 681 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — architecture-as-a-compiler lens: [docs/11-architecture-as-compiler.md](11-architecture-as-compiler.md).** Operator's framing: the project is essentially writing a compiler/interpreter. The doc maps each module to a compiler phase (parse→`Command[]` IR→semantic checks→topological evaluate→SVG codegen; replay = event-sourced rebuild; LLM = source-to-source desugarer; deterministic ids = hash-consing), notes where it's MORE than a compiler (constraint solver inside + non-deterministic output = model enumeration / the alternatives feature), and — the useful part — where new work slots in: **Phase 6 theorems = a static-analysis pass over the value-graph**, new constraints = solver cases (ADR-014), new input surfaces = front-ends onto the same IR, export = codegen back-ends. The enforced rule: keep phase boundaries clean (parser ⊥ coordinates; engine ⊥ which parser; renderer ⊥ engine internals). Use this lens when deciding where a feature belongs. Linked from docs/README + CLAUDE.md.

- **2026-06-13 — THE CORE FEATURE: accumulating constraints adapt the figure (ADR-028).** Operator: "this is the core element. this must work." Case: `C = AB∩DE`, then `C is the midpoint of OB` — the 2nd fact must MOVE the free chord so C lands on the midpoint, not be rejected as a redefinition. Built: a 2nd *placement* of an existing point P → reinterpreted in `applyStep` as `coincide(P, ~P)` (hidden `~`-prefixed target) that DRIVES a free 1-DOF ancestor of P (on-circle/on-segment, found by walking the dep graph back to the most-recent parametric point); solved at evaluate time by varying that DOF + re-evaluating the figure (a local-MINIMUM finder since coincidence dist ≥ 0). This is the deterministic 1-DOF driver (ADR-014) generalised — NOT a general numerical solver (keeps ADR-001's spirit: deterministic, enumerable branches, honest over-constraint on no-root). **Additive**: `evaluate = resolveDriven ∘ evaluateCore`; evaluateCore = old evaluate verbatim → all prior tests untouched. Verified: chord repositions so C is midpoint + on AB + on DE; fixed structure stays; no free DOF → honest over-constraint. Also fixed: English "midpoint of OB" read "of" as labels O,F. Limits: 1 DOF / 1 constraint at a time; alt roots not yet UI-cycleable; 2-DOF free points not driven. 685 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — half-parse fix: "diameter AB and chord DE meet at C".** Operator hit "unresolved dependencies for: C". Cause: `lineLineIntersection` matched "AB and DE meet at C" → `C = AB∩DE` but silently DROPPED `קוטר`/`מיתר` (diameter/chord), so A,B,D,E were never created (ADR-024 misparse, in the intersection rule). Can't be built offline because the diameter/chord need a circle id not named in this sentence (the implicit-circle-reference gap). Fix: a leftover guard — if `diameter|chord|radius|tangent|קוטר|מיתר|רדיוס|משיק` appears, `lineLineIntersection` returns 'stop' → escalates to the LLM (which has circle O as context and decomposes into diameter+chord+intersection). Plain "C = intersection of AB and DE" (operands pre-exist) still parses. 687 tests green. **Recommended next (recurring need): implicit-circle reference** — thread the figure's existing circle ids into `parse()` so unnamed circle constructs ("diameter AB", "radius is 8", "the circle") resolve to the single circle OFFLINE, no LLM.

- **2026-06-13 — coupled constraint solving (ADR-028 amendment).** Next case: `|ED| = 7` on top of `C = midpoint of OB` → "over-constrained: |ED|=7 cannot hold". Two causes: (1) the driver only drove on-SEGMENT carriers, so a constraint on two on-circle points (D,E) became a failing check — fixed: `driveOrCheck` now drives a free on-circle DOF too; (2) it's a COUPLED 2-DOF system (one constraint drives E, the other D, and they interact). Fixed: when ≥2 carriers, `resolveDriven` solves JOINTLY — coordinate descent (grid argMin per DOF → considers all branches) for a global start, then Nelder–Mead to converge out of the valley, minimising summed squared residual. Single DOF keeps the deterministic drivenRoots+branch path. Verified: chord through midpoint(OB) AND length 7 → both hold, D,E on circle. 689 tests green. Still open: 2-DOF free-point carriers; UI-cycling joint alternatives.

- **2026-06-13 — named foot vs altitude misroute.** "G is the foot of the perpendicular from E to AB" → "F is already defined". The `altitude` rule (perpendicular+from+to) grabbed it and AUTO-named the foot 'F' (ignoring the explicit "G is the foot"), colliding with the chord's F. The old coverage test passed only because altitude's auto-name happened to match the intended id (it never checked the id). Fix: `altitude` returns null when `foot`/`רגל` is present — an explicitly-named foot is the `foot` rule's job; altitude keeps only height/altitude + the bare unnamed "perpendicular from A to BC". Added id-level tests (foot keeps its name). 691 tests green. The operator's full corpus construction (circle, diameter, chord EF through C=midpoint(OB), |EF|=7, foot G from E to AB) now builds end-to-end.

- **2026-06-13 — figure orientation controls (rotate/flip), labels stay upright.** Added view-layer rotate 90°/180°, flip horizontal/vertical, and a manual rotation slider in `Figure.tsx`. Implemented as an ISOMETRY on world positions BEFORE the fit (`orient(v, {rot,flipX,flipY})` in `transform.ts`): the rotated figure is re-fitted+centred, labelDir is computed in the rotated frame, and because the `<text>` glyphs are never rotated (rotation is baked into positions, not the SVG `<g>`), labels stay upright automatically — exactly the "letters point up" requirement. Pure engine geometry untouched (renderer is the swappable back-end). View state (orientation+pan+zoom) persists across figure changes; Reset view clears it. 694 tests green (orient isometry unit tests added).

- **2026-06-13 — re-referencing a drawn line + "point E" misparse.** "draw tangent at D" then "point E is the intersection of the tangent at D and AB" → "tan-D is already defined". Two bugs: (1) the standalone tangent created line `tan-D` (visible); the compound re-creates `tan-D` (scaffolding) to intersect it → conflict (differs only in `visible`). A line's id IS its spec, so same id = same line. Fix: `commandConflict` allows line-over-line (reuse), and `addLine` OR-merges visibility (re-stating scaffolding as drawn upgrades it; like the circle upsert). (2) the result-point matcher in `tangentLineIntersection`/`parallelCircleIntersection` grabbed the 't' in "poin·t·" → named the point T. Fix: strip "point"/filler before matching the lone capital. Now: E on AB, on the tangent, tan-D stays drawn. 695 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — "inscribe"/"inscribing" escalated to the LLM (only "inscribed" parsed).** Operator typed an inscribed-triangle and saw it go through the LLM (steps showed a roundabout "circle through A B D" + "triangle ABD inscribed in circle O" decomposition) instead of one deterministic step. Cause: inscribedPolygon/incircle/SHAPE_LEFTOVER matched only `/inscribed/`, so the imperative "inscribe …" / "inscribing …" missed → escalated. Fix: broadened to `/inscrib\w*/` (inscribe/inscribed/inscribing). All forms now parse to one clean step (circle + on-circle×3 + triangle), circle drawn, no LLM. NOTE: the deterministic "triangle ABD inscribed in a circle" was never broken — verified end-to-end (parse/build/store-replay/SVG all draw the circle); only the imperative forms slipped. 695 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — draw the segments/lines a compound REFERENCES, not just the point.** Operator: "when we refer to sections, we should draw them and not just locate the point." The compound intersection rules created the referenced operands as invisible scaffolding (a `line-through` for AB, a non-visible tangent) and only placed the point — so the student saw E with no defining lines. Fix: those rules now DRAW what they reference — `tangentLineIntersection` (tangent visible + segment AB), `bisectorSegmentIntersection` (bisector visible + segment), `parallelCircleIntersection` (parallel line visible + segment), and `lineLineIntersection` (both referenced segments). Scaffolding `line-through` kept for the crossing (E may fall on an extension). Idempotent if the segment is already an edge. 698 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — "show another configuration" (FR-ALT) only partially worked.** Operator asked if the button works. It was wired but had gaps: (1) `branchId` (App.tsx) detected only `intersection`/`on-segment-solved`, MISSING the branchable circle points `arc-midpoint`/`line-circle`/`circle-circle` that `cycleAlt` can step → fixed to detect all four kinds. (2) `applySeed`/`freeDofs` only perturbed free-POINTS, so inscribed figures (vertices = on-circle, 1 free angle each) never varied — only the rigid center moved, which the fit normalised away → no visible change. Fix: mark on-circle points `free` when created at an arbitrary angle (auto-spread: inscribed triangle/quad vertex, chord/diameter end, tangency, arc end) vs fixed (explicit theta: inscribed square/rect/trapezoid corner); `applySeed` now slides free on-circle vertices by a seeded angle and `freeDofs` counts them. So resample gives a genuinely different inscribed triangle (vertices spread differently, still on the circle), while a fixed-angle square stays a square. Driven (ADR-028) and constrained on-circle points (have `solve`) are left alone. Still NOT wired: cycling the driven-constraint branches (the two chords through a point, the two tangent feet). 700 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — resample produced SLIVER inscribed triangles ("3 dots, no triangle").** Operator typed an inscribed triangle and saw 3 dots without edges. Cause: the just-added free-on-circle resampler perturbed each vertex independently by ±180°, which CLUSTERS two vertices → a ~2° sliver triangle (edges nearly invisible). A stale non-zero seed (from a prior "show another configuration") made the first view a sliver. The triangle edges themselves were always built+rendered (verified: 3 edge lines in the SVG). Fix: spread-PRESERVING perturbation — a single SHARED seeded rotation of all free on-circle vertices (keeps their spacing) + a small ±30° per-vertex jitter for variety; worst min-angle across 30 seeds went from 1.8° to 16.9° (always a proper triangle). 700 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — three tangent-figure fixes.** (1) `E = intersection of tangent at C and AB` didn't connect E to AB — the tangent meets AB on its EXTENSION, so the chord A-B didn't reach E. `tangentLineIntersection` now draws segments E-A and E-B (span the chord + its extension to E). (2) Visible lines (tangent/bisector/perp/parallel) were DASHED — operator wanted a regular line; renderer now draws them SOLID (`#334155`, no strokeDasharray). (3) `point F on the extension of AB` → "unresolved dependencies for: F" because `pointOnExtension` read "**of**" as labels O,F (the same filler bug as midpoint) → F=point-on-segment(O,F) referenced itself. Fixed: strip FILLER before reading the 2 segment labels (now F on AB, t=1.3). 701 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — auto-trim a visible line to the points on it.** Operator: "trim the tangent so only DE is shown, not the whole line." A visible `Line` (tangent/bisector/perp/parallel) is infinite, drawn clipped → spans the viewport. Fix (scene.ts): a visible line with TWO+ named points lying on it is now drawn as the SEGMENT spanning the extreme two (the tangent from its touch point D to where it meets the chord at E ⇒ segment D-E; a bisector from its vertex to the side it hits); with 0-1 points on it (a standalone tangent) it stays an infinite clipped line (no natural endpoints). Tolerance 1e-5 (line-intersection points sit on the line within float). General + automatic — works even when the unbounded tangent was drawn first, then E added. 701 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — trivial constraint "DF=DF" degenerate-drove F→A ("A and F at the same point").** Operator's step showed `DF=DF` (a typo for `DE=DF`). `DE=DF` works (drives E so |DE|=|DF|=5.48). But `DF=DF` → set-equal(D,F,D,F): same segment both sides, residual ≡ 0 for ALL carrier values; F was on-segment (from "F on the extension of AB"), so driveOrCheck drove F's t and solveParam picked t=0 → F=A → coincidence error. Fix: `driveOrCheck` now treats a same-segment equal/ratio (`{a,b}=={c,d}`) as a pure CHECK (residual 0 → passes; ratio k≠1 correctly fails), never a drive. So `DF=DF` is a harmless no-op. 702 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — a constraint must drive a FREE point, not one already pinned.** Operator: `DF=DE` failed "over-constrained: F coincides with ~F cannot hold", but worked after "show another configuration". F was defined TWICE ("point F on the extension of AB" + "F is the intersection…") → ADR-028 pinned F with a coincidence. `DF=DE` references F AND E (both on-segment); `driveOrCheck` picked the FIRST on-segment ref (F) and drove it, fighting F's pin → over-constraint (and at some seeds it happened to resolve, hence the resample working). Fix: `driveOrCheck` now prefers a carrier NOT already referenced by an existing constraint (a "pinned" point) → it drives the free E. `DF=DE` now holds (|DF|=|DE|=5.48, F not collapsed). 704 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — removing an early step now cascades honestly.** Operator: removing an earlier step should recalc with what's left and SAY if something's impossible. It already recalc'd (replay folds applyStep over the fact list) and flagged direct dependents — BUT a segment/shape would silently RE-CREATE a now-missing point via the auto-create-endpoints affordance (placeBase), masking the breakage (`segment AB` showed ✓ with phantom free-point A,B after the circle was removed). Fix (geoStore `replay`): track point ids any fact OWNS; a later fact that would re-create an owned point which isn't in the figure now FAILS with "can't build: X is no longer available (an earlier step it relies on was removed or failed)" instead of rescuing it. Owns set includes disabled facts (so toggling a dependency drops dependents, reversibly — ADR-010). Standalone `segment XY` (no owner) still auto-creates. 707 tests green.

- **2026-06-13 — implicit circle reference (ADR-029), fixes the recurring "spurious F" / LLM-escalation.** Operator: typed `המשיק בנקודה D והמשך AB נפגשים בנקודה E` (tangent meets AB-extension at E); it escalated to the LLM (no named circle) which invented a point F. Root cause = the parser was context-free. Fix: `parse(raw, ctx?)` with `ParseContext{circles:string[]}` (centre letters); `resolveCenter(s,ctx)` = named centre OR the one circle if exactly one present (0/2+ → null → escalate). Circle rules (tangent∩line, chord, diameter, arc-midpoint, parallel∩circle, drawn tangent) use it. Rewrote `tangentLineIntersection` to read BOTH phrasings + treat "extension of AB"/"המשך AB" as line AB. App passes `{circles: construction circles' centers}` to parse (submit + commitEdit). LLM prompt: "only introduce points the student names" (no invented intermediates). Now the case parses deterministically → tangent∩AB=E, no F, no LLM. Backward-compatible (parse() with no ctx unchanged). 711 tests green. Still context-free: point-on-circle, inscribed shapes.

- **2026-06-13 — recruit free DOFs when a constraint's carrier can't satisfy it (ADR-028 amendment 2).** Operator: `DE=DF` failed "cannot place F on segment AB so |DE|=|DF|" — F stuck on the segment, |DE| = far tangent length. The answer referenced the CURRENT placement; the engine should reconfigure to a possible one. Verified satisfiable (random search residual 4e-4). Fix: `applyStep`, when evaluate fails AND there's an unsatisfiable on-segment-solved carrier, calls `recruitFreeDofs` — walks the constraint's points back to their free on-circle/on-segment ancestors (`freeCarrierAncestors`), turns the stuck carrier + those ancestors into joint `solve` carriers for the constraint, and the coupled solver reconfigures the figure. Result: DE=DF holds, F on segment AB, A,B,D on circle. PURELY ADDITIVE — only fires after the normal solve fails (712 tests green). Limit: recruits via point-parents; line-intersection's line-deps aren't walked (here D,A,B are reached via D + F's segment ends anyway).

- **2026-06-13 — "diameter AB" on existing points = constraint (AB is a diameter), + multi-constraint joint solve.** Operator: `קוטר AB` after building the inscribed triangle → "B is already defined" (diameter wanted to recreate B as A's antipode, conflicting with the existing vertex). Fix: `reinterpretDiameter` (step.ts) — when both endpoints already on the circle, reinterpret as `coincide(midpoint(A,B), centre)` + segment, marking an endpoint as a driven carrier (so A,B become antipodal). Also: `resolveDriven` coupled path now DEDUPES the constraint set (JSON key) so a NEW constraint on already-driven DOFs isn't outweighed by a constraint shared across many carriers. Result: `diameter AB` makes the vertices antipodal (|AB|=2r, midpoint=O), AND it composes with the existing DE=DF — the unified solve satisfies BOTH (|AB|=10 diameter AND DE=DF) by reconfiguring. 714 tests green. This is the multi-coupled-constraint frontier starting to compose (still per-constraint carrier marking, not a fully unified solver).

- **2026-06-13 — inscribing an EXISTING triangle = its circumcircle (was "A and O at the same point").** Operator made `משולש ABD` (free triangle) then `משולש ABD חסום במעגל` → error. Cause: inscribedPolygon created a NEW circle (centre O auto-placed at origin) + tried to put A,B,D on it (no-ops since they exist as free points) → the new O landed on vertex A (also at origin) → coincidence. Right meaning: when the vertices already exist, "inscribe in a circle" is the CIRCUMCIRCLE through them. Fix: extended `ParseContext` with `points` (existing point ids); inscribedPolygon, for a triangle whose 3 vertices all exist, emits `circumcircle` (ADR-025) instead. App passes `parseCtx()={circles, points}`. A NEW triangle (vertices absent) still places them on a fresh circle. ParseContext fields made optional (backward-compat). 714 tests green. Still: quad inscribed with existing vertices not handled (only triangle).

- **2026-06-13 — UI: commands panel defaults CLOSED + "align segment horizontal".** (1) `showHelp` now defaults false (the command-reference panel is collapsed; "מה אפשר להקליד?" opens it). (2) Added a small input to the Figure orientation bar: type a segment's two endpoints (e.g. "AB") + Enter → the view rotates so that segment reads horizontal (`rot = −atan2(B−A)`, lays A→B along +x; the Y-flip preserves horizontal). A pure VIEW operation (engine geometry untouched), so the student picks a reference segment instead of nudging the rotation slider. One-time set (re-type to re-align if the figure changes). 715 tests green.

- **2026-06-21 — Q4 tangent/secant trapezoid draws cleanly (seeder fix, [ADR-078](06-decisions.md#adr-078)).** Operator session `m26xv4m2`: quad BKCD with KB∥CD, CD chord, KB tangent at K, BD secant cutting the circle at A. **Construction that works** (type in order; the tangent–chord theorem is the trick — KB tangent at K ∧ KB∥CD ⟺ K = arc-midpoint of CD): `מעגל סביב O רדיוס 5` → `מיתר CD במעגל O` → `K אמצע הקשת CD במעגל O` → `A על מעגל O` → `B חיתוך המשיק למעגל O בנקודה K עם AD` → `מרובע BKCD` → `E חיתוך AK ו-BC`. It always built geometrically-exact, but the clean textbook drawing (convex BKCD **and** A between B,D) was ~1/300 under the seeder; root cause was `sample.ts` keying the on-circle jitter width off the *total count* of free on-circle points (chord C,D + secant A = "3 → tight ±30°", so CD never got short). Fixed by scoping tight jitter to genuine inscribed polygons → ~100× more clean configs; reachable via "show another configuration" (which cycles the arc branch + reseeds — the parser defaults arc-midpoint to branch 0 = A-beyond-D, so a couple of presses are needed to land branch 1 with A between). 1205 green. Deferred deterministic options (constrain A onto segment BD / trapezoid-first) noted in the ADR.

- **2026-06-21 — implicit circle auto-created + a live-Haiku reality check ([ADR-079](06-decisions.md#adr-079)).** Live-tested the LLM fallback on the verbatim Q4 problem (operator authorised one Haiku call via `POST /api/parse` on the dev server). Haiku decomposed it in **reading order** (general quad first, NO arc-midpoint insight — exactly as predicted) and treated the circle as *given*: it emitted the chord/tangent/secant steps but **no step creating circle O**, so the build collapsed (undefined centre → "unresolved dependencies"). Fix: the parser now prepends a free `circle-O` (free centre + radius, `ifAbsent` so it never clobbers a real circle) whenever a circle-consuming step references a circle absent from context. The exact decomposition now builds; the leftover "C not on circle" is the deeper trapezoid-first reorder gap (ADR-073), correctly flagged amber by the verifier — not silent-wrong. **Method note:** the `POST /api/parse` curl (utterance = whole problem, context = "The canvas is empty.") is the sanctioned way to see where live Haiku lands — only with operator authorisation. 1209 green.

- **2026-06-22 — the noun/definitional form of line∩circle ([ADR-096](06-decisions.md#adr-096)).** Operator asked if "נקודה E היא מפגש של AO עם המעגל" ("point E is the meeting of AO with the circle") is supported — it returned `not-handled` → LLM, even though every **verb** form ("AO חותך/פוגש את המעגל בנקודה E", "AO meets the circle at E") already built the right `line-circle-intersection`. Two gaps in `lineMeetsCircle`: (1) `INTERSECT_KW` had the verb meeting forms but not the **noun** `מפגש`/`meeting` (note `חיתוך`/`intersection` already passed via the `intersect` substring); (2) `crossingAfterCircle` only finds a point named *after* the circle, but here E is declared *first*. Fix: accept the noun keyword + a new `leadingNamedPoint` helper that reads the result point named ahead of the construction (`[נקודה] E היא …` / `point E is …`), mirroring the existing line∩line noun handling. All seven phrasings (verb+noun, He+En) now build identically. **1243 green, build clean.** Locked by the `point-is-meeting-of-line-with-circle` scenario.

- **2026-06-22 — convex-by-default is a SOLVER preference, not just a seeder rule ([ADR-097](06-decisions.md#adr-097)).** Operator figure: `מרובע BCED חסום במעגל` + external `A=BD∩CE` + `AE=2CE` + `AD=CE` drew a CROSSED (bowtie) quad while the cue read "5 DOF" and "show another configuration" said impossible. Root cause: the general inscribed quad pinned its vertices at the convex-default angles (`free=false` — an [ADR-052](06-decisions.md#adr-052) violation), so the constraint solver could only move E,D and was boxed into a crossed branch (a convex BCED satisfying the constraints exists only when ALL four vertices are free — confirmed numerically, ~half the family is convex). Three coordinated fixes: (1) `point-on-circle` gains a `free` flag and the general-quad vertices are FREE (the convex angles are a STARTING position; shaped polygons stay fixed); (2) `solutionAccepted`/the driven solvers run a **convex-required pass first**, falling back to relaxed only if no convex solution exists (no-op when no ≥4-gon); (3) on a convex-failing coupled solve, `resolveMixedCarriers` RECRUITS the polygon's free vertices as extra DOF and retries convex. Principle (operator-confirmed): *convex is the default whenever a convex drawing satisfying the givens exists* — concave only when the givens force it (a stated reflex angle / point-inside) or an explicit opt-in. Now: convex BCED, both constraints hold, verifier green, `freeDofs` includes B,C → 12/16 resample views convex (was 0). **1245 green, build clean.** Locked by `constrained-inscribed-quad-stays-convex` + `constrained-inscribed-quad-resample`. Deferred (no corpus need yet): the concave OPT-IN — a stated reflex angle (precise) and/or the `קעור` keyword (FR-EN-16 sugar).

- **2026-06-24 — a tangency against an EXISTING circle is a constraint, never a re-built circle ([ADR-115](06-decisions.md#adr-115)).** Operator session `fn2wt71w`: kite ABCD, triangle BCD inscribed in circle O, then `AB ו AD משיקים למעגל O` → `'O' is already defined`. **First move was the right one — the log-class audit, not the fix.** Scanned `logs/debug-log.jsonl` for the whole `'X' is already defined` error class (11 distinct sequences), reconstructed each, and **replayed them against HEAD**: 9 were already fixed by later ADRs (107/099), and the hard crash itself was a **stale dev server** (the running server predated the ADR-107 placement fix — hence the operator's "restart server" instinct). On HEAD the crash was gone but the figure was **silently wrong, verifier amber** (`cornerTangentCircle` rebuilt circle O — free centre on the bisector + `circle-through` the foot — re-radiusing it so the inscribed B,C,D fell off). The audit surfaced a **sibling**: `incircle` ("משולש DEF חוסם את המעגל O") did the same to an existing circumcircle. Root cause (both): the rule auto-named the circle's centre from the utterance without checking it already exists. Fix: an existing-circle guard on each — `cornerTangentCircle` emits a per-arm tangency CONSTRAINT (`set-perpendicular(O, tip, vertex, tip)`, tip on circle); `incircle` builds the DUAL (3 free touch points on O → tangent at each → named vertices as the pairwise tangent intersections, deterministic). **Trap caught:** the naïve incircle inversion (foot of ⟂ from O onto each side, forced onto the circle) showed `violations: []` but **over-constrained the per-constraint driver on the third side** — missed because I checked `violations` but not per-step `status`. A green verifier is necessary, not sufficient. Locked by scenarios `corner-tangent-on-existing-circle` (operator's exact sequence) + `triangle-circumscribes-existing-circle`. **1326 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-24 — arc-measure term + implicit-circle tangent ([ADR-116](06-decisions.md#adr-116), [ADR-115](06-decisions.md#adr-115) Am.).** Operator manual test on bagrut Q4 (session `6ai22ulh`), two gaps. (1) **No `קשת`/arc term** — the textbook given `⌢DE = 2⌢CE` (arc DE = 2·arc CE) couldn't be typed. Fix: `arcEquality` rule — an arc's measure = its central angle, so arc XY ≡ ∠XOY and the relation becomes `set-angle-ratio` on the central angles (the same engine relation as `∠ABC=∠DEF`); bilingual `קשת`/`arc`/`⌢`, equality or ratio, circle resolved implicitly. (2) **Spurious E** — the student typed `AB ו AD משיקים למעגל` with NO name; the ADR-115 fix only caught the *named* circle, so the unnamed tangent (one circle present) fell through to the build-a-new-corner-circle path and created tangent feet E, K + an auxiliary circle P, **hijacking the label E** ("placed where I didn't expect, won't move"). Fix: the existing-circle guard now resolves the circle implicitly (named OR the one circle, ADR-029), so the unnamed tangent constrains O and creates no points. With both, the clean Q4 build verifies and arc DE = 2·arc CE holds (∠DOE = 2∠COE). Method: re-derived the book's intended construction as the Opus oracle (no live API) and confirmed end-to-end through `parse → replay`. Locked by scenario `arc-ratio-and-implicit-tangent-q4`. **1327 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-24 — equilateral/isosceles qualifier on an inscribed triangle ([ADR-117](06-decisions.md#adr-117)).** Operator manual test (session `dhhj7wo3`): `ABC משולש שווה צלעות חסום במעגל` (equilateral triangle inscribed in a circle) drew a GENERIC inscribed triangle — the equilateral qualifier was silently dropped. Root cause: `inscribedPolygon` handles quad shapes (square/rhombus/… via fixed angles) but ignored the triangle shape word, and "שווה צלעות" is not a `SHAPE_LEFTOVER` token so it neither constrained nor escalated; the standalone macros defer to `inscribedPolygon` for the inscribed case, so nothing added the equal sides. Fix: `inscribedPolygon` detects equilateral/isosceles and appends the macros' equal-side constraints (equilateral = two adjacent pairs equal; isosceles = soft |AB|=|AC|) in every branch, so the on-circle vertices flex into shape (verified |AB|=|BC|=|CA|). Plain inscribed triangle unchanged. Locked by scenario `equilateral-triangle-inscribed` + parser-coverage. **1332 tests green, build clean.** (Also noticed but NOT in scope: the worded arc form "קשת DE שווה פעמיים קשת CE" still escalates — `arcEquality` requires the `=`/numeric-coef form, not the spelled-out "שווה … פעמיים".)

- **2026-06-24 — AREA as a first-class measure & constraint ([ADR-118](06-decisions.md#adr-118)).** Operator asked to support area after a manual "area of BDE = 13" question; scoped by reading how area is *given* across two real bagrut PDFs (worksheet + the 2025 `571` booklet). Built end-to-end: `polygonArea` (shoelace) + `area`/`area-ratio` constraints (solve.ts residual/scale/tol) + `set-area`/`set-area-ratio` via `driveOrCheck`; a `measure-area` symbolic command lowered like `measure-length` (shared variable → ratio; lone `SABC = S` → free label); one `area` parser rule (registered BEFORE the shape rules) handling compact `SABC`, verbose `שטח [ה<shape>] ABC` / `area of …`, and the RHS forms — value/radical/variable/power, ratio (`/`, `פי k`, `רבע/שליש/חצי`, `היחס…הוא r`), and the `נסמן…ב-S` letter label; verifier re-derives it; centroid label; DOF cue. **Key principle (scale vs shape, the answer to the operator's earlier question):** a LONE absolute area only pins the figure's SCALE (invisible after the viewport fit, ADR-101) — an equilateral triangle stays equilateral, just resized; area reshapes only as a ratio or a 2nd absolute given (same as an absolute length). `similarityGauge` counts an absolute `area` as scale-fixing so the DOF cue doesn't double-count. **Two Hebrew traps:** JS `\b` doesn't fire around non-ASCII (so `הוא`/`רבע` matched without it); area refs read only the LEADING vertex run (else the literal word "area" is misread as labels A,R,E,A). All formats from both documents parse + build verified. Locked by scenarios `area-absolute-sets-scale-not-shape` + `area-ratio-reshapes` + parser-coverage; catalogued. **1344 tests green, build clean.** *Deferred: cross-kind coupling (`area = p²` with `OE = p`) and full trig-expression RHS.*

- **2026-06-26 — tangent draws no 90° mark + a redundant constraint may lend its hoarded carrier ([ADR-129](06-decisions.md#adr-129), [ADR-130](06-decisions.md#adr-130)).** Two operator-reported items in one session. (1) **No right-angle square on a tangent** — a tangency lowers to `set-perpendicular(O, T, vertex, T)` (radius ⟂ tangent at T), and `angleMarkFor` drew a 90° square at every touch point, violating the rule "mark only a STATED right angle, never a computed one." Fix: an `implicit?: boolean` flag on `set-perpendicular`, set by the tangent emitters, suppresses the mark; a typed `AB ⟂ CD` still marks. (2) **A solvable figure falsely "over-constrained" with a misleading red "pending — add givens" message** (session `rw2ypbgq`): kite ABCD, circumcircle O of BCD, AD&AB tangent, E on arc BC with arc BE=2·arc EC, then `E על המשך DO` (D,O,E collinear ⇒ E = antipode of D). Every free DOF was claimed (tangencies→A,B; kite→C,D; arc→E); the collinear found none free, and the recruiter's steal only fires for an over-subscribed (≥2-carrier) constraint. The system is solvable only because the kite's AB=AD is **redundant** (implied by the two equal tangents from A). Confirmed differentially (dropping the tangents makes the same `set-line` solve). Fix: **case (E)** in `recruitFreeDofs` lends a reachable claimed carrier to the new constraint and accepts the first lend under which the WHOLE system evaluates valid — **self-verifying** (a lend that breaks its old constraint fails `evaluate` and is rejected, so it can never draw a wrong figure; sidesteps the reverted global multi-start). Operator chose "prototype + validate before commit"; validated (figure builds — E antipode of D, all four on O, arc ratio holds — full suite green, no perf hit) then finalized. Locked by scenarios `segment-tangent-no-explicit-touch-point` (no 90° mark) + `collinear-flexes-redundant-carrier-kite-tangents`. **1389 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-26 — an inscribed trapezoid carries AB ∥ CD as a constraint, not just fixed angles ([ADR-131](06-decisions.md#adr-131)).** Operator session `su2xwopc`: `טרפז ABCD חסום במעגל` → tangent-meets-line `E` → `BE=BC`, and "in the third step the engine forgets it's a trapezoid." Root cause: the inscribed trapezoid's `AB ∥ CD` lived ONLY in the fixed starting angles `[215,325,60,120]` (non-free vertices, no `set-parallel`), so `BE=BC` drove an on-circle vertex (driveOrCheck's on-circle case ignores the `free` flag) off its angle and the parallelism was lost. Same class as ADR-117 (inscribed triangle's equilateral/isosceles qualifier dropped — shape lived in placement, not a relation). Fix: emit a persistent `set-parallel(A,B,C,D)` via `shapeCmds` AND make the trapezoid's vertices FREE (`freeAngles` includes trapezoid) — the base ratio/height are unstated DOFs (ADR-052), so the figure flexes to satisfy later givens while AB ∥ CD persists; cyclic + parallel ⇒ isosceles automatically. Square/rect/rhombus stay angle-fixed (rigid up to similarity). Locked by scenario `inscribed-trapezoid-stays-a-trapezoid-when-flexed`. **1390 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-26 — an unlabeled polygon auto-names its vertices ([ADR-132](06-decisions.md#adr-132)).** Operator session `lag0hgpa`: "מרובע חסום במעגל doesn't work." Root cause: every polygon rule required an explicit vertex-label run, so a bare shape (`מרובע חסום במעגל`, `quadrilateral inscribed in a circle`, standalone `square`/`משולש`/`דלתון`/`regular pentagon`) fell through the deterministic parser to the LLM — only `circle`/`מעגל` (no vertices) worked bare. But a bare shape is a *common simple* input the offline parser should own. Fix (operator chose broadest scope, "all polygons everywhere"): when a shape rule finds NO labels and nothing else geometry-significant left over, it auto-names vertices A,B,C,… (skipping existing points) via shared `autoVertexLabels`/`shapeLabels`, wired into `quadShape`/`triShape`/`shapeMacro`/`inscribedPolygon`/`regularPolygon`. Two guards: a PARTIAL run (`מרובע ABC`, 3 labels for a quad) still escalates (not auto-completed), and a leftover (circle/constraint) defers to the right rule. The "named?" test is `namesVertices = /[A-Z]/` — vertex labels are uppercase Latin, cleanly separating a named vertex from lowercase English prose (`inscribed in a circle` → `in a`) and from a multi-letter run `labelRun(…,1)` misreads. Locked by `auto-label.test.ts` (7) + scenario `unlabeled-inscribed-quad-auto-names-vertices`. **1398 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-26 — page UI: attribution footer, fit-to-viewport, examples that all build ([ADR-132](06-decisions.md#adr-132) Am.).** Operator requests, UI-only. (1) Added an **attribution footer** ("פותח על־ידי ד\"ר דוד קודיש · לשאלות: david.codish@gmail.com", email a `mailto:` link; i18n He/En, RTL). (2) The footer pushed the page past `100vh` → scroll; fixed by widening the canvas height budget `calc(100vh - 132px)` → `calc(100vh - 180px)` (the magic offset the codebase already uses) and dropping the footer's `marginTop:auto`, so header→canvas→footer fits without scrolling for a normal figure (a long step list still scrolls by design). (3) The first-page example chips must each BUILD ("1 doesn't and 1 breaks" — a bare segment isn't a shape, and `נקודה E על AC ב-45%` errored on a non-existent AC); reset `examples.items` (both locales) to self-contained builders (`טרפז ABCD חסום במעגל`, `מקבילית ABCD`, `שני מעגלים נחתכים`, `ריבוע ABCD`, `משולש ABC`, `מעגל O ברדיוס 5`). `שני מעגלים נחתכים` didn't parse, so `twoCirclesMeet` now auto-names the crossings A,B (ADR-132 principle). Guard `examples-build.test.ts` replays every example in both locales. **1402 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-27 — Reveal / relations layer BUILT (first slices) + a tangent-extension direction fix ([ADR-134](06-decisions.md#adr-134), [ADR-135](06-decisions.md#adr-135)).** Operator chose to build the "view relations" feature (FR-RV). **Engine (`src/engine/relations.ts`, read-only consumer, ZERO engine edits — only additive exports):** `detectRelations(construction)` samples the figure across its free DOFs (`applySeed`+`evaluate`) and keeps only relations invariant across ALL samples (ground truths, not drawing coincidences). Slices: **equal sides**, **equal angles**, and **definitive angle VALUES** (an angle the same in every sample → its degrees; scale-invariant, so e.g. equilateral shows 60° even though size floats; straight/degenerate excluded). Negatives are the load-bearing tests (scalene triangle → nothing). **GUI:** store `viewRelations()`/`clearRelations()` cached against the `facts` ref (auto-clears on any fact change, SURVIVES "show another configuration" since ground truths are config-invariant); scene `relationMarks()` → world-space ticks/arcs/value-labels; Figure renders tick hatches + concentric arcs + bisector value labels (white halo). Operator-driven refinements: **per-vertex radial staggering** of value labels (no overlap at busy vertices); **composite-angle suppression** (drop ∠ABD when ∠ABC+∠CBD are shown — the student reads the total from the parts, value-sum test); **per-equality-class COLOURS** (a palette so overlapping equal-angle arcs are distinguishable). **ADR-135 (separate bug surfaced while testing):** `המשך AB` meeting a tangent put E on the WRONG extension — `tangentLineIntersection` built E as a `line-intersection` of the infinite line with no order. **A first attempt (global `set-line` order enforced via the sampler) broke 4 tests + blew up to 300 s — reverted.** Correct fix = the proven ADR-127 `order` field, now added to `line-intersection`: a `collinear-order` whose residual folds into the joint solve flexes the figure so E lands beyond B in the default config (no sampler, no perf hit). The operator's "it worked before, why a big change?" was right — the rule never guaranteed the side; the small `order` field does. Locked by scenarios `tangent-meets-extension-lands-on-named-side` + the relations tests (`relations.test.ts`, `relations-layer.test.ts`, `relation-marks.test.ts`). **1424 tests green, build clean.** Remaining Reveal slices: definitive LENGTHS (only when scale-anchored) + side/area ratios, then the similar/congruent-triangles side list (FR-RV-4/5).

- **2026-06-29 — a NAMED altitude segment keeps its foot name ([ADR-149](06-decisions.md#adr-149)).** Operator manual test: asked for **CD** to be a גובה (altitude); the figure built but the segment came out **CF**. Diagnosis (the test wasn't in `logs/debug-log.jsonl` — logging interrupted — so reproduced from the description): the `altitude` rule derived the apex only from a "from/מ" phrase and **always** auto-named the foot via `freeLabel` (→ F). A leading *named* altitude segment ("CD גובה …", C=apex/vertex, D=foot on the opposite side) matched no apex phrase → `not-handled` → escalated to the LLM, which rephrased to "altitude from C" → the parser auto-named the foot F → `segment CF`, silently dropping the student's D (an ADR-052-adjacent label override). Root fix: the `altitude` rule recognises a leading named altitude segment (regex `^<L><L> (is the|הוא|ה)? (height|altitude|גובה)`) → `apex` + `namedFoot`, and the foot id is `namedFoot ?? freeLabel(...)`. **Scoped to the height/altitude keyword only** — the generic perpendicular-from form is excluded because `altitude` runs *before* `perpendicularConstraint` in the rule order, and "EF אנך ל AB" must stay the ⟂ constraint (a named-foot branch on `אנך` would steal it and fabricate a spurious foot). An altitude is never a constraint, so "CD גובה" is unambiguous. Verified end-to-end (foot D on AB, CD⟂AB, no fabricated F). Locked by `phase4.test.ts` (5 assertions incl. the anti-hijack guard) + scenario `named-altitude-keeps-its-foot-name`. **1517 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-29 — named-derived-point audit ([ADR-150](06-decisions.md#adr-150)).** After fixing the named-altitude bug (ADR-149), the operator asked "are there other similar issues?" — so the bug *class* was audited, not just the report. The class: **a rule auto-names a derived point and discards a name the student gave.** Method: enumerated every `freeLabel(...)` call site and classified each. Most already honour a named form (`named ?? freeLabel`; circle centres / tangency / secant points), and **median** + **angle-bisector** already read a student-named endpoint (the correct model). Two real findings: (1) **midsegment** auto-named both endpoints (M,N) with no named path → "PQ קטע אמצעים …" renamed P,Q to M,N (the true sibling); (2) the ADR-149 altitude fix only caught the name-FIRST order — the **keyword-first** order ("הגובה CD" / "the altitude CD") still escalated → renamed the foot to F (the original CF symptom). Both fixed by reading a named pair in either word order. **The trap this surfaced (worth remembering):** letting labels follow the keyword made a keyword-first regex read the lowercase connector word "to" as two single-letter labels (`t,o`→`T,O`; "in"→`I,N`), so the *unnamed* "the midsegment to BC" produced endpoints T,O. The clean discriminator is **case** — vertex labels are uppercase (`namesVertices = /[A-Z]/`), connectors are lowercase; a shared `isUpperLabel` helper gates every named-segment capture (the keyword needs the `i` flag, so `[A-Z]` can't be enforced inside the regex — validate the captured token instead). **Deferred (lower priority, logged in the ADR):** a foot-first letter order ("DC גובה", foot before vertex) still escalates — `altitude`/`median` both assume the leading letter is the apex, matching the Israeli textbook vertex-first convention. Locked by `phase4.test.ts` (named-altitude + named-midsegment blocks) + scenarios `named-altitude-keyword-first-keeps-foot` / `named-midsegment-keeps-its-endpoint-names`. **1531 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-06-29 — named altitude, the SECOND root cause: a context fallback for the opposite side ([ADR-149](06-decisions.md#adr-149) Am.).** After the named-segment regex fix, a live retest STILL produced `CF`. The debug log (session `243yyqae`) showed why: the operator typed the bare **"CD גובה"** (triangle ABC already on the canvas, no side/triangle in the utterance), which went to the LLM → `גובה מ-C במשולש ABC` → foot F. The regex was matching `CD` fine, but the rule then **bailed because it required the opposite side to be stated** and had no `ctx`/figure-context fallback — so the most natural phrasing (triangle already drawn) escalated and lost the foot. The `median` rule already derives the side from context; `altitude` didn't. Fix: `altitude` now takes `ctx` and falls back to the figure's points (the two that, with the apex, are the only ones), exactly like `median`; the whole-string label scan for the compound "triangle ABC with a height from A" is preserved (a regression caught by parser-coverage — removing the old `|| s` fallback broke it). **Lesson (worth keeping): a parser fix verified only on the explicit/stated phrasing is not done — check the bare context-dependent form a student actually types (the triangle is already on screen, so they DON'T restate it). The named-segment regex was necessary but not sufficient.** Also a reminder of the stale-server trap: the first retest that "still showed F" was partly because two stale dev servers (Jun 25 + Jun 28) were running — killed them and started one fresh on :5173 before this diagnosis. Scenario `named-altitude-keeps-its-foot-name` rewritten to the exact bare sequence (`משולש ABC` → `CD גובה`). **1533 tests green, build clean.**

- **2026-07-03/04 — theorem ground-truth corpus reviewed & locked (Phase 6 gate).** The operator reviewed a 12-item HTML checklist (an interactive review page built the prior session, `scratchpad/gt-review.html`) against the printed 571 booklet and returned verdicts; all twelve are now adjudicated in [sample questions/theorem-ground-truth.md](sample%20questions/theorem-ground-truth.md) (resolved-checklist section) and the four pedagogy calls folded into [16-theorems-plan.md](16-theorems-plan.md) §10. Transcription checks: **B8, B11, B17, B22, B23 CONFIRMED**. **B5 REMOVED** ("remove this question" — its exam's live geometry question stood in for a red-X'd trapezoid Q4; dropped rather than substitute → the trapezoid family 39–42 stays uncovered, corpus now **25 usable questions**). **B2 RESOLVED** — "M is the circumcenter of △BDC" is a GIVEN, so #98 surfaces the moment M is typed (no post-ב demotion). **B12 — the sharp finding: a source typo, kept ∥ not ⊥.** The operator's verdict was "WRONG — it shows perpendicular" (the booklet part-ב glyph reads ⊥). Rather than flip the corpus to a geometrically FALSE statement, re-read PDF p125 + re-derived the figure: the givens force the unique inscribed order A=180°,B=0°,D=240°,C=300° where **OC and AD are the identical −60° vector (exactly parallel)**, and the exam's own part ג (prove CE tangent ⇒ CE⊥OC, with CE⊥AD given) is satisfiable ONLY if OC∥AD — ⊥ would need a crossed quad. Concluded the compilation booklet has a **print typo in part ב**; corpus keeps **∥**, prominently flagged for the operator to optionally cross-check the original exam PDF. (Honoured "report faithfully / surface contradictions" over blind compliance.) **Pedagogy calls:** **B0** — relevancy is NON-monotonic (a theorem's rank can DECREASE as later givens arrive, re-assessed every step — a ranking rule over the current full fact set, folded into D3) + Appendix-(O)/definitions MAY appear as clearly-separated SUPPLEMENTAL content (softens the "structurally impossible" line; the corpus test still asserts O ∉ the primary feed). **B1** — the tool NEVER sees the question text, so there is no "target shape" concept; **detected shapes (`detectShapes`) drive theorems** (a kite's 37/38 fire when a kite is detected, e.g. B14 once BDGE is built), which revises D5's "detectShapes is not an input to matchers." ⚠️ **Boundary flagged for the 6a feed review (NOT decided here, per the ask-when-unsure-of-scope rule):** does "detected shape" include a purely emergent shape or only one whose defining points were all explicitly constructed? Corpus entries assume the latter. **B2c** — #87 surfaces GREEN whenever ≥4 points are stated on a circle (drawn quad NOT required; detectShapes emits "מרובע חסום במעגל" for the concyclic set); applied to B9, B13 (amber→green). **B3c** — no sub-question exception needed ("we will never know what the question is, so there is no dilemma"). Docs-only session (no engine/src touched). **The corpus is now the Phase-6 gate; 6a starts on Q5–Q7.**

- **2026-07-04 — a GENERIC triangle earns no shape badge ([ADR-221](06-decisions.md#adr-221)).** Operator (latest manual test): "the trapezoid and several triangles were not detected in the shapes. In general, if there is nothing special about a triangle we don't need to show it (otherwise there are too many)." Investigated the logged sessions first: **trapezoid detection was never broken** — a declared `טרפז ABCD` / inscribed cyclic trapezoid surfaces its badge in every logged figure (base- and store-path alike; even a `טרפז ABCD`+`AC` diagonal gives exactly `[trapezoid:ABCD]`). The actionable half is the triangle flood. `detectShapes` was inconsistent: an EMERGENT generic triangle was already dropped (`if (t !== 'triangle')`), but a DECLARED generic triangle (a 3-vertex `polygon`) always badged. Fix: the declared branch mirrors the emergent one — only a forced special type (isosceles/equilateral/right/right-isosceles/30-60-90) badges. **Nothing lost for theorems:** `table.ts` reads typed `triangle` commands straight from `ctx.facts` (and `structuralTriangles` recognises drawn-edge 3-cycles), both independent of the badge list; the badge's only unique contribution was a redundant copy of a declared triangle. Locked by `detectShapes.test.ts` (the old "generic badge still surfaces" assertion flipped to absent) + scenario `generic-triangle-gets-no-badge` (`טרפז ABCD`+`משולש ABD` → trapezoid badges, plain triangle does not). Theorem+scenario (497) & detectShapes (20) suites green, `tsc -b`+`vite build` clean. **Follow-up (same report, the REAL miss):** the operator's screenshot clarified they meant *"there is also a trapezoid ABCE"* — a parallelogram ABCD with CD extended to E (CD=DE) + EA, where AB ∥ CE makes ABCE a trapezoid. It was genuinely undetected: the emergent-cycle edge graph had only ATOMIC edges, and the trapezoid's side C–E is broken by D, so the 4-cycle A-B-C-E was unenumerable. This is the DUAL of ADR-167's `collinearSplits` — nothing MERGED a collinear chain into a through-edge. Fix: a new `collinearMerges(c, samples)` adds the through-edge C–E when D is strictly between C,E and collinear in every sample (fixpoint for longer chains), wired into `detectShapes` enumeration only (scoped — a through-edge duplicates an existing ray in the angle universe). Trapezoid ABCE now detected; generic triangle ADE stays unbadged. **A SECOND bug the seed-sweep oracle then caught:** at base seed 2 the trapezoid was still dropped — one internal sampler config drew ABCE *thin* (a real ~0.3° corner, `sin≈5e-3`) which fell under `hasStraightVertex`'s tolerance of `sin(0.5°)≈8.7e-3` and was misread as a collinear (phantom) vertex. `hasStraightVertex` (ADR-198) had reused the ambient shape `angleTol` (~0.5°), which is ~5 orders of magnitude too loose for "collinear" (a phantom vertex is collinear to solver precision ~1e-10). Fixed with a dedicated `STRAIGHT_SIN_TOL=1e-4` — still kills phantom kites (collinear ≪1e-4) but accepts a genuine narrow corner. Locked by scenario `emergent-trapezoid-through-a-point-on-its-side` (seed-swept). **Lessons: (1) my first AskUserQuestion assumed the miss was the triangle; the operator's one-line correction ("there is also a trapezoid ABCE") pointed at the real gap — confirm WHICH shape before designing a policy. (2) A "collinear/degenerate" tolerance must be set by solver precision, NOT reused from a shape-classification angle tolerance.**

## Resume pointer

See the **Status** line at the top of [09-implementation-plan.md](09-implementation-plan.md), the **2026-06-11 (home PC, cont.) — Phase 5c** entry just above, and the "Current state / Next step" section in [../CLAUDE.md](../CLAUDE.md). Branch `rebuild-foundation`; GitHub remote `dcodish/geo_builder` (push after committing — Dropbox `.git` is sync, not backup, and corrupts). Sanity check: `npx vitest run` (expect **173 green**), `npm run dev`. **The full corpus Q1–Q7 is reproduced; v1 construct vocabulary is complete.** **Next milestone: Phase 6a (theorem feed) — the ground-truth corpus is operator-reviewed and gating (2026-07-03/04); build against Q5–Q7 first.**

- **2026-07-06 — the operator-commissioned review + ADR-231 (doctrine + fixes).** The operator: "some changes are not robust enough… performance is also an issue… I want a review and options" → a three-agent review (robustness of ADR-228/229/230, measured profiling, detection-layer architecture), then "fix the issues… and write the design rules that prevent patching in the future." **Review verdicts:** the recent solver commits were accumulating special-case routing (two probe-verified holes on HEAD: Q11 order-dependence F1, silent perimeter drop F5); performance measured (one "view relations" press = ~45 s on the ADR-123 figure, ~85% in failing solves; `resample` unbudgeted at ~28 s; theorem feed cleared at <2 ms); **client-server for detection REJECTED** — the right split is main-thread vs Web Worker + one shared sample set (server adds RTT + the Plesk fragility and removes no compute). The same morning prod session `fn34ptei` hit `'O' is already defined` both ways on one figure — the 5th member of the ADR-075/099/115/119/124 class. **Shipped (ADR-231):** `docs/17-design-rules.md` (the doctrine: class-first diagnosis, patch tripwires, chokepoint registry, mechanisms M1–M4, perf rules, escalation template — linked from CLAUDE.md with operator authority); M1 existing-id lowering (reinterpreters widened to any point kind + stated order kept + conflict-branch recruit + honest relation errors + `solutionAccepted` carrier-degeneracy guard); M2 ownership/order (structural deferrable predicate, unowned-coincide re-home, (F)-bake directive restore, minimal-first staged recruiting, the HOIST dual of ADR-104 — **Q11 sizes-last now builds to the closed form**); M3 perf (evaluate WeakMap memo, one shared budgeted sample core for relations+shapes, determined-figure single-sample, resample budget); honesty F5/F6/F8. **Scoped OUT, operator to green-light:** full F4 routing unification, orphan re-home beyond coincide, deleting keepTangencyDriven, solver early-outs, the Web Worker split (backlog). Lesson for the log: the general mechanisms mostly EXISTED (ADR-028/050 reinterpretation, ADR-104 deferral, withOrderCons) — the bugs were their GATES (param-carrier-only, hand lists, missing failure paths); repairing a mechanism beats adding a case. Also: over-recruiting DOFs is not just slow, it CHANGES ANSWERS (the minimal 4-D solve converges where the 8-D marking diverges) — recruit minimally, verify, widen.

- **2026-07-06 — save/load a figure file (FR-HS-10 realised) + the fixtures regression net ([ADR-232](06-decisions.md#adr-232)).** Operator: "save a drawing and upload it from a file… will also help manual testing… perhaps even automatic regression testing?" Built exactly as FR-HS-10 specced (the fact list IS the file — no positions): `src/store/figureFile.ts` serializes `{schemaVersion, locale, savedAt, seed, radiusOverrides, facts, display}`; facts keep BOTH the utterance (human-readable) and the lowered commands (load replays commands — an LLM step never re-escalates; `cmd.branch` keeps the alternative); the `seed`+dialed radii ride in the header so the saved CONFIGURATION reloads (they live outside the facts — the detail the FR's one-liner missed); display styling travels for the author audience. `loadFigure` is ONE `set` → one undo restores the pre-load session; `deserializeFigure` refuses bad/newer/empty files with a bilingual note and tolerates hand-edited files; the App smoke-replays before committing. Sidebar gained שמירה/טעינה לקובץ buttons + a hidden file input. **The testing payoff:** `src/__tests__/fixtures.test.ts` replays every `fixtures/*.geo.json` through the real load path — build-green + verifier-clean (ADR-053 makes that strong) + a parser-drift differential (stored utterance must still lower to the stored commands) — so a manual session's saved figure becomes permanent regression coverage by dropping the file in the folder. Seeded with 4 corpus figures (defining interaction, equilateral-inscribed, parallel-chords, trapezoid-altitude) generated through the real pipeline and verified green before writing. **Correction recorded:** the ADR-043 "on-segment-solved branch not persistable" note was already resolved 2026-06-17 (`branch?` on `point-on-segment`) — the round-trip test proves a cycled point reloads at its cycled position. **2299 tests green (2 pre-existing env-gated skips), `tsc -b` + build clean.** **COMMITTED + PUSHED + DEPLOYED (commit `dfd9359`)** — client-only change, static `dist/` → `httpdocs/geo-builder/` (live bundle `index-CDSeMmHJ.js`), proxy untouched (healthz ok).

- **2026-07-06 — an explicit equality that names a shape's SOFT default pins it (commit), not "already drawn" ([ADR-234](06-decisions.md#adr-234)).** Operator session `z4v1zza3`: `משולש ABC שווה שוקיים` drew, "show equal length" showed nothing equal (correct — the pair is unstated), then `AB=AC` — the student SAYING which sides are equal — errored "already exists." Diagnosed from the log through the real parse→replay path: the engine WAS enforcing `|AB|=|AC|` at every seed (verified to machine precision; the seed-0 default is near-equilateral, base/leg 1.041, which is *why* the operator couldn't see it — but that's a free-DOF default, not the bug). The bug: the isosceles bakes a SOFT default apex-A pair (ADR-138); `AB=AC` names that same pair, so the figure doesn't move, so `dryRunOutcome` read `empty` and the gate dropped it as "already on the figure — nothing to add." **Class (M4):** *a student's explicit statement that coincides with a soft default is swallowed instead of applied as the student's choice* — stating the pair is genuine new info (soft→forced: it flips from unreported/cyclable to reported/pinned) even with no coordinate delta. **Fix:** `dryRunOutcome` gains a semantic "produced" case (sibling of `reveals`/`dataOnly`) — a `set-equal` naming an enabled shape-variant's not-yet-stated pair counts as produced (pure `pinsSoftVariant` in `shapeVariants.ts`; both kite/isosceles, mirrored order, excludes already-stated pairs + the pairless midsegment; cost stays off the common path). Now: isosceles → nothing forced; `AB=AC` → commits (no move, ADR-138 suppression) → "show equal length" reports the pair. Apex ANGLE stays free (varied by "show another"); only the PAIR becomes the student's. **Filed not fixed (out of scope):** a duplicate explicit `set-equal` isn't deduped by the construction, so re-typing `AB=AC` a *second* time grows the constraint count and shifts the figure ~0.75 — a constraint-dedup concern, not this gate; the single-statement report is unaffected. Locked by `pins-soft-variant.test.ts`, `dry-run.test.ts`, and scenario `isosceles-pin-soft-pair` (App.submit-faithful gate, the operator's exact sequence). **2314 tests green, `tsc -b` + build clean.** **Lesson:** the first AskUserQuestion assumed the near-equilateral LOOK was the bug; the operator's clarification ("I didn't say which are equal … AB=AC is to say which — there is an error") relocated it to the commit gate. Confirm the operator's model before designing the fix.

The unstated touch↔circle **pairing is a `softPair` default** (stated order) a `replay` pre-scan SWAPS when a later explicit membership names the opposite assignment (M4 defaults-yield, the ADR-163 pre-scan shape) — exactly what the operator's own session needed ("tangents from N to O1 at M and B" puts B on O1, the reverse of the default). Shipped (all in `src3d/`, zero `src/` touches): **claims as a first-class fact kind** — the student's ANSWER, verified against the figure across FOUR seeded configurations (`claims.ts`; a statement true only in one drawing of a free-dim figure is a coincidence → refuted), wrong answers **refused keep-prior** (`claim-refuted`), verified ones badge ✓; claim forms `vec-eq` (`AM = ½u + ½v + 5/3w`, fraction glyphs + `5/3` + decimals), `perp-plane` (`CA' מאונך למישור BC'D`, proof-prefix `הוכיחו כי` tolerated), `collinear3` (`על ישר אחד`); **`נסמן: AA' = w, KC = v, KB = u`** → name-vector bindings (arrows stripped; the נסמן/denote keyword is what separates naming from a claim — both contain `=`); **centroid** (`מפגש התיכונים של משולש`, draws its triangle); **the 2020-ב span point `P על AM כך ש-KP = αu + βv`** — Greek scalars = unknown coefficients, so it DEFINES P: the complement coefficient of one Cramer decomposition is AFFINE in t → the drive is one division (closed form; the D3 no-CAS boundary held — no Gram machinery even needed, evaluated coordinates suffice); guarded by `need-basis` at apply, a `checkInSpan` post-check (`no-solution`/`not-on-segment` — על means ON the segment, the 2-D ADR-077 principle) refused keep-prior, and the **parser tripwire**: `spanPoint` before `onSegment` + a Greek hard-guard in `onSegment`, so a span condition can never silently degrade to a free point (§6 honesty); **auxiliary segments auto-draw** (claim pairs, the ⊥ segment + plane triangle, centroid triangle, span carrier — idempotent `segment3`, parser-side macro lowering, engine stays dumb; bare `AM`/`קטע AM` draws too) and **dash by the textbook interior rule** (midpoint strictly inside a solid, or on only back-facing faces — on the 2023 cube CA′ and BD dash, BC′ stays solid, matching the printed exam figure). - **2026-07-06 — 3-D V2 BUILT (the algebraic lane, [ADR-3D-004](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-004)) — GATE MET: 2022-Q2's FULL constructive chain reproduces He+En.** Operator "lets move to next step". The chain, typed: `המישור π1: z-3=0` → `המישור π2: ay+z-8=0` (ONE symbolic parameter per figure, `LinExpr` coefficients, a second letter refuses `two-params`) → `הזווית בין המישורים היא 45` pins `a` by **grid-scan + bisection root-finding** (D3-compliant; roots snapped to clean integers; roots = the figure's BRANCHES; no roots = `no-roots` honest refusal, e.g. a stated 95°) → `A(2,-2,6) נמצאת על אחד המישורים` — **the membership given SELECTS the branch** (a=−1; satisfiable in no branch → `not-on-plane` refusal; without membership the seed cycles a=±1 on "show another"; an unpinned parameter is a SAMPLED free DOF per ADR-052) → `מ-A מורידים אנך למישור π1 החותך אותו בנקודה B` (closed-form foot, drawn + ⟂ knee mark) → `AB = 3` scalar claim ✓ → `ℓ ישר החיתוך בין המישורים` (plane∩plane, **echoed on-canvas in parametric form** `ℓ: x = (0, -5, 3) + t·(1, 0, 0)` — the exam's הצגה-פרמטרית ask answered by the figure) → foot C on ℓ → `שטח המשולש ABC = 4.5` ✓. Hand-worked oracles asserted exactly (B=(2,−2,3), C=(2,−5,3), anchor (0,−5,3)). Architecture: **`resolve3`** supersedes bare positions (positions + numeric planes/lines + the parameter's fate; renderer consumes the whole `Resolved3`); the algebraic overlay (light axes sized to the figure, translucent per-plane patches that never occlude, the clipped drawn line, ⟂ knee marks) renders only when the figure is Lane A, and the isotropic fit includes it. **Honest lane boundary:** a numeric size claim on a free-dim SOLID figure is a scale statement, not a check — refused `size-on-solid`. **Parser trap for the log: `ℓ` (U+2113) is not a `\w` char, so `\b` after a line name NEVER matches** — explicit lookaheads (caught by the gate scenario). **110 src3d tests green; full suite green; `tsc -b` + `build` + `build:3d` clean.** **Operator screenshot follow-up ("the visualization of planes is critical — no way to see the line between the planes or where 45 is"), fixed at root ([ADR-3D-004](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-004) Am.):** patches had floated apart (each centred on its own centroid projection); now **intersecting planes' patches share a focus point ON their fold with a patch axis ALONG it** (crossing geometrically guaranteed on screen), **the fold draws as an implicit seam before ℓ is named** (the named line replaces it), and **a stated plane-angle gets a dihedral arc + its value at the seam**. Locked by the screenshot-state scene test. **Second follow-up ("point A didn't show on one of the planes — extend the plane to cover points on the plane"):** patch extents now grow asymmetrically (fold-anchored) to COVER every point lying on the plane, seam stretches with the union; a point off the plane never inflates a patch (negative assertion). Locked by point-in-patch-quad tests on the 2022 chain. **Then, operator "tested and looks good. I want to add the save as and load feature which will help me a lot in testing" → save/load BUILT ([ADR-3D-005](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-005), the ADR-232 pattern transplanted):** portable `.geo3.json` (utterance + lowered commands + seed + enabled flags; NEVER positions; orbit camera excluded — a view concern), strict total deserialisation (`bad-file`/`newer-schema`/foreign-app refusals, fresh ids), **load = ONE undoable set**; שמירה/טעינה buttons. **The testing payoff: the `fixtures3/` regression net** — every `*.geo3.json` dropped in the folder replays through the real load path asserting all-facts-OK + NO parser drift (stored utterance still lowers to stored commands); seeded with the three gate figures via the env-gated generator (`GEN_FIXTURES3=1`); an empty net fails loudly. 125 src3d tests green, `tsc -b` + `build:3d` clean. **⟵ RESUME the 3-D track with V3 (parameters in LINE directions + point components, typed parametric lines `x=(−1,5,−11)+t(m−1,5−m,−2)`, ∦-for-every-m probes — gate: 2024-Q2).** **COMMITTED + PUSHED (`ee3bd23`, selective staging again — the concurrent session's T2/ADR-243 WIP left untouched, PROJECT-MEMORY staged as a synthetic blob since both edits shared one hunk) and DEPLOYED: bundle `3d-Ct5jh-eb.js` → httpdocs/3d-builder/, live 200 (V2 + plane-viz fixes + save/load now public).**

- **2026-07-06 — T2 first tranche ([ADR-243](06-decisions.md#adr-243)): 87/102 concyclic evidence + medians 15–17 + congruence 18–21 + midsegments 62–67 + bisectors 75/78/80 — 66/109 tabled (was 43).** The widest T1 evidence gap closed at the spine's hint layer (`buildMatchCtx` enriches circle members from `concyclic` constraints; the 87 matcher gains the operator's B2c ≥4-members path) — 87 flipped gaps→expect in 7 questions + 102 in B19. Three families tabled with per-family evidence helpers in `table.ts` (`medianFacts`/`medianGroups`, `midsegmentFacts` declared-L1 + joined-stated-midpoints-L2, `bisectorStatements` with the arc-equality exclusion, `congruenceEvidence` constellations). **Two design lines the guards themselves enforced:** (1) a SHARED side does not count toward a congruence constellation — a draft auto-paired it and #20 fired on B8's kite halves (exactly ה's step); (2) a `set-angle-ratio` k=1 at a circle-CENTRE vertex is arc talk (ADR-116), not a stated bisector. **Corpus hardening:** b-corpus now asserts `lastError === null` per question (`knownBuildIssue` bucket) — exposed B13's real engine finding ("line GB meets circle O at D" fails '|GA| = |AC| cannot hold' though constructible; ADR-229/230 class, filed). 79 → needs-construct (point-to-LINE distance); 77/81 stay planned. 16 new matcher locks. Full suite green, `tsc -b` clean. **Next: T2 remainder + leftover evidence worklist (see ADR-243 tail).**

- **2026-07-06 — 3-D V3 BUILT (parameters in lines, [ADR-3D-006](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-006)) — GATE MET: 2024-Q2 reproduces He+En** (after committing/deploying the V2 batch: `ee3bd23` + deploy note `0835468`, live bundle `3d-Ct5jh-eb.js`). The chain, typed: `הישר ℓ: x = (-1,5,-11) + t(m-1, 5-m, -2)` (a TYPED parametric line — `Line3Def` gains a parametric kind, LinExpr components via `parseParamExpr`, one letter per figure) → `המישור π: 3x + my + (m+6)z + 4 = 0` (parseLinearEq handles PARENTHESISED coefficients all-or-nothing; bare π names allowed) → `ℓ אינו מקביל ל-π לכל m` — **the never-parallel probe is a claim over the parameter FAMILY** (parallel iff dir(m)·n(m)=0; the claim holds iff the normalised residual has no zero over the scan — sign changes AND near-zero touches refute; the exam family −m²+6m−15<0 for all m verifies, a refutable plane refuses) → `הישר ℓ ניצב למישור π` pins m: **a ⟂ residual |dir×n| is NON-NEGATIVE (touches zero, never sign-changes), so paramRoots gains a minima-scan + ternary-refinement root finder beside bisection, and candidate roots are CROSS-FILTERED against ALL pinning givens** → m=−5 unique → `ℓ חותך את π בנקודה A` (closed form; **parallel at the chosen parameter → refused `line-misses-plane`, never a fake point**) → `A = (2, 0, -10)` coords-eq claim ✓ (wrong coords refused) → `B(5,-5,-9) על הישר ℓ` — on-line membership VERIFIED (`not-on-line` when false): the acceptance itself resolves the exam's ד trap (the point is ON ℓ, so infinitely many planes contain both). **Recorded trap: a parser poison marker written as a quoted space reached the file as a NUL byte and broke exact-match editing — markers must be visible characters (§).** 145 src3d tests green; vite build:3d clean; tsc clean on src3d (the tree's only type errors at close were the concurrent session's in-flight src/parser edit). **⟵ RESUME the 3-D track with V4 (the coordinate-injection pivot: gauge-free Lane-G figures + injected coordinates, symbolic point components A(3,n,p), sign branch givens — gate: 2020-ג + 2023-ג–ה; then ALL FOUR corpus exams reproduce end-to-end).**

- **2026-07-06 — two CONCENTRIC circles ([ADR-244](06-decisions.md#adr-244)) — circle identity decoupled from the centre letter (bagrut Q6).** Operator: "שני מעגלים בעלי מרכז משותף O gives me just one circle" (log `3k5jezuu`: the LLM's two-step decomposition collapsed into a RESIZE of the one representable `circle-O`; the En form half-parsed to one circle; "המעגל החיצוני/הפנימי" silently attached to the one circle). Class fix at three seams: the **pair macro** (`circle-O` bound OUTER + `circle-O-2` bound INNER via `set-radius-order` — role binding at creation is pure gauge, both radii stay free DOFs, `ifAbsent` keeps a pre-stated size), the **qualifier-resolution post-pass at the parse seam** (the ADR-119 chokepoint pattern — every circle rule gains חיצוני/פנימי/גדול/קטן + outer/inner/larger/smaller at once; stated membership disambiguates unqualified refs; otherwise a new `ambiguous-circle` clarification à la ADR-164 — never a silent pick or LLM guess), **`circleMembers` re-keyed by circle id**, and the **radius-order requirement riding the existing verifier gate** (`figure.v.radiusOrder`; `meetsRequirements` already gates on a clean verifier, so the sampler/"show another" skip order-violating radii for free). Sizing rides ordinary distance givens (`OA=4` — ADR-230's routing was already per-membership); a paired centre's radius sliders label themselves outer/inner. Siblings fixed with it: the `CARRIER_RELATION_TAIL` comparative misread ("במעגל הגדול" read as a length relation on the chord), the LLM-context members-map overwrite, the store's per-centre extension lookup. Filed: `theorems/context.ts` maps members to circle objects BY CENTRE (a pair's inner entry gets the outer's id in MatchCtx — feed fidelity nit on concentric figures only; that file is the T2 session's live edit). Locked by scenario `two-concentric-circles-q6` + `concentric.test.ts` (25) + a catalog entry (+`concentricCircles → circle` in the shadow allowlist — the coarser rule reading the same words as ONE circle is the exact bug). Full suite green (2724); `vite build` clean (`tsc -b` red at close only on the parallel session's in-flight src3d files). **COMMITTED + PUSHED (`1bba137`, selective staging — the T2 session's ADR-243 WIP left untouched; the shared docs staged as synthetic HEAD+mine blobs) and DEPLOYED (2026-07-06): built from a detached HEAD worktree (the working tree carried the T2 session's uncommitted theorems WIP — never shipped; HEAD-state theorems+concentric tests re-run green in the worktree first), bundle `index-BGVeJg9w.js` → `httpdocs/geo-builder/`, root:root 755/644 — LIVE at themathbible.com/geo-builder/ (page + asset checked 200).**

- **2026-07-06 — 3-D V4 BUILT (the coordinate-injection PIVOT, [ADR-3D-007](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-007)) — GATE MET, and with it THE MILESTONE: ALL FOUR corpus exams (2020/2022/2023/2024) reproduce end-to-end from typed He/En utterances** (the docs/20 "Q1–Q7 reproduced" moment for the 3-D tool; V3 committed first as `90483f1`). The plan's hardest slice, shipped: **a coordinate statement about an EXISTING point is a GIVEN, never an error** (the 2-D M1 principle — `point3` on a taken id lowers to a pivot PIN; partial `A(3,n,p)` pins only numeric components; a NEW point with letters refuses `symbolic-new-point`); `נתון: v = (10,-5,0), u = (5,5,-5), P(0,4,6)` parses as ONE injection list. **The pivot is a numeric similarity+dims least-squares** (`solve3.ts`): Levenberg–Marquardt with a central-difference Jacobian over translate+axis-angle+log-scale+the solids' free dims, deterministic 8-rotation seed-rotated multi-start with restart-polish (~1e-24 err); a vector pin transforms without translation. Numeric solving is the 2-D engine's own category — the D3 no-CAS boundary holds. **Under-determination is welcome (ADR-052):** the 2020 prism height is never injected — LM's damping lands on a nearby manifold point per seed, so "show another configuration" varies exactly what the givens never fixed while K=(−3,4,7) stands still (locked in the gate). **Reflection is the discrete branch:** both mirror orientations solved; sign givens (`שיעור ה-z של C' חיובי`) select among them (else the seed cycles — locked: resample flips C′.z=±5); `sign-unsatisfiable` / `injection-unsatisfiable` refuse keep-prior. **Planes THROUGH POINTS** (`plane-through`, Newell normal from post-pivot positions, a second line-resolution pass) render patches and feed `plane-plane-line` — the 2023-ד ℓ resolves through B and C′; the **`plane-eq` claim** (`המישור KBC: x + 2y + 3z - 26 = 0`) verifies the student's equation (scalar multiples pass; degenerate sets refuse). **Engineering note recorded: the claim tolerance is set by the pivot's numeric floor** — a central-difference Jacobian bottoms out ~1e-6 in loosely-conditioned UNPINNED directions (A.z returned −6e-6 at some seeds), so claim REL_TOL moved 1e-7 → 2e-5: far above the noise, orders of magnitude below any wrong bagrut answer (≥0.5 off). Hand-worked oracles asserted: K = P − (u+v)/5 = (−3,4,7); plane KBC normal ∥ u×v ∥ (1,2,3), d=−26; A=(3,−4,0) unique (n=−4, p=0); C′=(4,3,5). 2023-ה (find-a-plane) deferred as planned — a construction ask the tool doesn't yet express. **169 src3d tests green; full suite green; `tsc -b` + `vite build:3d` clean.** **⟵ RESUME the 3-D track with V5 (corpus widening ~10 more Q2s from the 806/807 papers as scenario gates, catalog panel, responsive canvas, the LLM fallback behind the proxy, pyramid done properly, image export); V6 solids-trig (committed scope, operator D4) after.** **COMMITTED + PUSHED (V4 = `29a61a6`; V3 = `90483f1` earlier) and DEPLOYED: bundle `3d-CF88lHvM.js` → httpdocs/3d-builder/, live 200 — the public tool now carries V0–V4, the full four-exam corpus.**

- **2026-07-06 — 3-D V5 + V6 BUILT ([ADR-3D-008](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-008) + [ADR-3D-009](06b-decisions-3d.md#adr-3d-009)), operator "do V5+V6 one after the other and commit and deploy".** **V5 (breadth + polish):** a corpus agent read 6 MORE 572 exams (10 total) — **the headline discovered gap, filed as V7: a point defined by a general VECTOR COMBINATION** (`DF⃗ = (k/2)·DB⃗ + k·DC⃗`, `CF⃗ = k·CD⃗`, `A'K⃗ = 4/5·DN⃗` — 4 of the 6 exams, usually with an unknown coefficient later pinned by a ∥-plane condition; siblings filed: solid-on-axes phrasing, skew justification); **2019 קיץ became the V5 gate** with small root additions — `line-through` two points (resolved from final positions + a FINAL line∩plane fill pass), the colon ratio `AE:EC = 2:1`, the angle-between-segments claim (undirected ≤90°), the length-ratio claim — K = ℓ(A′C)∩plane(BC′D) = (2,4,2) verified He+En. Plus: **right pyramid** (`פירמידה ישרה` — apex above the base's CIRCUMCENTRE, equal lateral edges locked by test; oblique refuses per ADR-052); **catalog3** (the coverage map + in-app commands panel + a guard test that EVERY entry parses He+En); **the LLM fallback with NO new infrastructure** — the SHARED proxy picks the 3-D prompt by a `tool: '3d'` body field (`server/parseHandler.ts` is the one place binding both apps; src/ and src3d/ still never import each other), `llmShared3` builds the prompt from the catalog, the client re-parses every returned line deterministically (`submitSteps`, all-or-nothing), PAR-10 contract test re-parses every prompt example; responsive canvas (ResizeObserver), PNG image export (SVG→canvas 2×), and the DOF cue (`freeDofCount3` — pivot-adjusted dims+7−pins). **V6 (the D4 committed solids-trig block):** corpus reality check — cylinder/cone/sphere NEVER appear in 572's Q2 (they belong to the 571 paper; validation filed), so the block ships against formula-sheet canonicals: the `revolution` construct (unstated radius/height = FREE sampled DOFs, stated numbers pin; unnamed centres stay UNNAMED — no invented labels), sampled-outline rendering (horizontal circles split front-solid/back-dashed by the eye azimuth, silhouette generators ±(ẑ×eye), the sphere's EXACT orthographic silhouette, dashed axis/height), and volume/lateral-area/surface **π-claims** (`נפח החרוט = 100π`, `שטח המעטפת = 65π`, `שטח הפנים של הכדור = 36π`) verified from the formula sheet with honest APPLY-time refusals (`no-such-solid`, `free-size-claim` — a size value on a free-size solid is a scale statement, never "refuted"). Gates: 2019 He+En, cone/sphere/cylinder chains He+En, DOF cue 2→0. **270 src3d tests green; full suite green; `tsc -b` + `vite build:3d` + `build:proxy` clean.** **⟵ RESUME the 3-D track with V7 (vector-combination points + coefficient pinning); also filed: 571-paper reading for the revolution block.** **COMMITTED + PUSHED (`5dc0014`) and DEPLOYED: static bundle `3d-D8qDsyZq.js` → httpdocs/3d-builder/ (live 200) AND the SHARED PROXY redeployed for the tool-switch (backup `proxy.mjs.bak-20260706`, `systemctl restart geo-proxy`, healthz ok, route 405-for-GET through Apache) — NO live Haiku call fired (operator policy: only the operator authorises one; the 3-D fallback's first live test is theirs).**
