The HTML escape hatch (ADR-251) stays unbuilt; FR-HS-11 is fully closed.

- **2026-07-10 — shared-endpoint המשך either-side semantics unified into `extensionsClear` ([ADR-267](06-decisions.md#adr-267), issue #19).** Operator re-tested the ADR-124/#6 source question (booklet 571 p.78 Q4) in the dev env (session `eew5ezi5`) and E landed BETWEEN C and A ("fails to create the C-A-E sequence") with every step ✓. Root cause: ADR-142's either-side rule for shared-endpoint extensions lived only behind a `relax` flag in `firstSatisfyingSeed`'s fallback pass — the strict primary sweep demanded the geometrically-impossible letter-order side at every seed and burned the app's 2500ms budget before the fallback, AND `meetsRequirements`/`findValidConfig` used the strict form so `autoResolve` rejected the very seed the fallback found. Suite never saw it: test mode runs `SEARCH_BUDGET_MS = Infinity`. Fix (after a first cut — either-side as the unconditional default — was reverted by the ADR-098 fallout it caused: on a free-DOF figure the letter order genuinely SELECTS the config): a PREFERENCE LADDER — strict wherever achievable, the ADR-142 either-side bar as the acceptance tier, searched in ONE interleaved sweep; `meetsRequirements`/`findValidConfig`/`resample`/the sample filter honour it; locked by scenario `shared-endpoint-extension-either-side-default` + a production-budget (2500ms, cold-replay) `findValidConfig` lock. Search on this figure: 5.7s → ~150ms. Also this session (triage): the #6 source located (both tangencies ARE givens; the ADR-124 contradiction was a D-for-E letter reuse), ADR-251 docx gate PASSED by the operator.

- **2026-07-11 — fix session: the P1 trio from the jsptarcl triage ([ADR-275](06-decisions.md#adr-275) #36 · [ADR-276](06-decisions.md#adr-276) #37 · [ADR-277](06-decisions.md#adr-277) #30) + a new radius-symbol triage (#53/#54).** (1) **#36:** `tangentLineIntersection` bound touch/cut by POSITION (first post-משיק בנקודה label), so the book's "דרך הנקודה C העבירו משיק … חותך את המשך הקטע BA בנקודה E" swapped the roles and refused; `orientTouchCut` now binds the touch to the circle MEMBER wherever it sits (positional only as the both-new tiebreak, through-carrier breaking it); sibling `twoTangentsMeet` defers when its "meet" label is a member (geometrically impossible); the rule's label reads moved to the UPPERCASE convention (En word-tails no longer read as labels). (2) **#37 — two solver class fixes:** a SATISFIED one-sided order's aim-margin residual competed at full weight in the joint costs and dragged the minimum off a hard equality's root (`jointCostTerm` zeroes a satisfied order — shared by both joint solvers); and the failure path gained **stage-0 `settleOnFrozenPrior`** (M2 minimal-first on the primary seam): the new statement's own carriers solve over the FROZEN prior solution before any recruiting, directives restored per the ADR-229/F2 law. The operator's 10-step figure: **24.6s refusal → 0.86s green**. Blame honesty: a final over-constrained refusal names the student's NEW statement, never a collateral casualty. (3) **#30:** a bare pair × circle crossing ("GB חותך את המעגל בנקודה D") now carries the ADR-127 `order:[a,D,b]` within-segment default (ADR-077/268 class, line∩circle edition); `הישר`/`line` keeps infinite-line semantics (B13 asserted unchanged); sibling `lineCutsCircleTwice` swept. (4) **Triage (report mid-session, filed not fixed per docs/22 §2b):** the booklet question's part ג — `משולש ADO חסום במעגל שרדיוסו r` **silently builds a bare triangle** (the inscription vanishes; with a comma the circumcircle builds but `r` is dropped — no honesty-gate lane for lowercase measure symbols) → **#53 (P1)**; radius symbols as first-class measures (naming a 2nd circle's radius, `רדיוס המעגל השני הוא T`, the ratio `R/r = 2√7/5`) → **#54 (P2 feature)**. Scenarios: `tangent-through-on-circle-point-binds-touch-by-membership`, `tangent-rider-collinear-solves-own-offset`, `bare-segment-cuts-circle-keeps-on-segment-default` + `adr-276.test.ts` + phase4 class tests.
